dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAturan untuk AI Agent:\u003c/strong\u003e endpoint \u003cstrong\u003ePOST\u003c/strong\u003e dan \u003cstrong\u003ePUT\u003c/strong\u003e pesanan \u003cstrong\u003ewajib\u003c/strong\u003e\nmemakai \u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003eContent-Type: multipart/form-data\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e. \u003cstrong\u003eJANGAN PERNAH\u003c/strong\u003e mengirim\nbody JSON dan \u003cstrong\u003eJANGAN\u003c/strong\u003e memakai \u003ccode\u003eContent-Type: application/json\u003c/code\u003e untuk\nendpoint ini. Kirim setiap parameter sebagai form field terpisah dengan nama\npersis seperti di bawah. File desain dikirim sebagai \u003cstrong\u003emultipart file field\u003c/strong\u003e\n(bukan Base64, bukan JSON). Boundary \u003cstrong\u003etidak\u003c/strong\u003e ditulis manual — biarkan\nHTTP client/library yang membuatnya otomatis. Pastikan semua field wajib\ntersedia sebelum mengirim request; jika ada yang kurang, tanyakan dulu\nkepada user, jangan kirim request.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv milik atau \u003ccode\u003eAdmin\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003ePetugas\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e—\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePUT\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e/api/v1/pesanan/{id}\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePemilik, hanya bila \u003cstrong\u003ebelum ada pembayaran\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emultipart/form-data\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePUT\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e/api/v1/pesanan/{id}/status\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eAdmin\u003c/code\u003e (ubah status pengerjaan)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eapplication/json\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDELETE\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e/api/v1/pesanan/{id}\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePemilik, hanya bila \u003cstrong\u003ebelum ada pembayaran\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e—\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePembayaran yang dibatalkan \u003cstrong\u003etetap tercatat\u003c/strong\u003e di tabel \u003ccode\u003epayments\u003c/code\u003e, sehingga\npesanan yang sudah pernah dibuatkan pembayaran tidak bisa lagi diubah (\u003ccode\u003ePUT\u003c/code\u003e)\natau dihapus (\u003ccode\u003eDELETE\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eDELETE /api/v1/pesanan/{id}\u003c/code\u003e adalah \u003cstrong\u003esoft delete\u003c/strong\u003e: baris \u003ccode\u003ePesanan\u003c/code\u003e dan\n\u003ccode\u003ePesanan_Detail\u003c/code\u003e tidak dihapus permanen, hanya diisi \u003ccode\u003eDeleted_At\u003c/code\u003e /\n\u003ccode\u003edeleted_at\u003c/code\u003e. Setelah di-soft delete, pesanan tidak muncul lagi di endpoint\nGET mana pun dan \u003ccode\u003eGET /api/v1/pesanan/{id}\u003c/code\u003e mengembalikan \u003ccode\u003e404\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" server\u003c/strong\u003e\nberdasarkan \u003ccode\u003eproduct.pricingMode\u003c/code\u003e + konfigurasi harga. \u003ccode\u003eunpaid\u003c/code\u003e (belum ada pembayaran) atau salah satu status\npembayaran (\u003ccode\u003epending\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003epaid\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ecancelled\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eexpired\u003c/code\u003e, dst — lihat\n\u003ca href=\"/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/docs/README.md#status-pembayaran\"\u003eREADME\u003c/a\u003e).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNilai tersebut diambil dari \u003ccode\u003epayments.idStatusPayment\u003c/code\u003e pembayaran terakhir\npesanan (JOIN ke tabel master \u003ccode\u003estatus_payment\u003c/code\u003e), lalu kodenya dipetakan di\nC# lewat \u003ccode\u003ePaymentStatusMap\u003c/code\u003e. Pesanan yang belum punya baris di \u003ccode\u003epayments\u003c/code\u003e\ntetap \u003ccode\u003eunpaid\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eKetiga endpoint pembacaan (\u003ccode\u003eGET /api/v1/pesanan\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eGET /api/v1/pesanan/all\u003c/code\u003e,\n\u003ccode\u003eGET /api/v1/pesanan/{id}\u003c/code\u003e) sekaligus menyinkronkan pembayaran yang masih\n\u003ccode\u003epending\u003c/code\u003e ke Midtrans (\u003ccode\u003eGET /v2/{order_id}/status\u003c/code\u003e) sebelum response dikirim.\nJadi status pesanan yang baru dibayar langsung berubah menjadi \u003ccode\u003epaid\u003c/code\u003e pada\nrefresh berikutnya, tanpa menunggu notifikasi webhook. Bila Midtrans tidak\ndapat dihubungi, daftar pesanan tetap dikembalikan dengan status yang tersimpan\ndi database.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003estatusPengerjaan\u003c/code\u003e bernilai \u003ccode\u003eSedang Berlangsung\u003c/code\u003e, atau \u003ccode\u003eDibatalkan\u003c/code\u003e bila\npembayaran pesanan dibatalkan oleh pelanggan atau kedaluwarsa (lihat\n\u003ca href=\"/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/docs/payment.md#post-apiv1paymentidpesanancancel\"\u003epayment.md\u003c/a\u003e).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" Pesanan","","Base path: `/api/v1/pesanan` — semua endpoint **butuh Bearer token**.","`idUser` diambil dari JWT, bukan dari body.","","\u003e Endpoint mana yang dipakai frontend admin dan mana yang dipakai pelanggan:","\u003e lihat [admin.md](admin.md) dan [pelanggan.md](pelanggan.md).","","\u003e **Aturan untuk AI Agent:** endpoint **POST** dan **PUT** pesanan **wajib**","\u003e memakai **`Content-Type: multipart/form-data`**. **JANGAN PERNAH** mengirim","\u003e body JSON dan **JANGAN** memakai `Content-Type: application/json` untuk","\u003e endpoint ini. Kirim setiap parameter sebagai form field terpisah dengan nama","\u003e persis seperti di bawah. File desain dikirim sebagai **multipart file field**","\u003e (bukan Base64, bukan JSON). Boundary **tidak** ditulis manual — biarkan","\u003e HTTP client/library yang membuatnya otomatis. Pastikan semua field wajib","\u003e tersedia sebelum mengirim request; jika ada yang kurang, tanyakan dulu","\u003e kepada user, jangan kirim request.","","## Ringkasan Endpoint","","| Method | Endpoint | Akses | Content-Type |","|---|---|---|---|","| POST | `/api/v1/pesanan` | Pelanggan (pemilik) | `multipart/form-data` |","| GET | `/api/v1/pesanan` | Pesanan milik sendiri | — |","| GET | `/api/v1/pesanan/history` | Semua role — filter tanggal/status/paginasi (lihat [laporan.md](laporan.md)) | — |","| GET | `/api/v1/pesanan/all` | `Admin` / `Petugas` | — |","| GET | `/api/v1/pesanan/{id}` | Pemilik, atau `Admin`/`Petugas` | — |","| PUT | `/api/v1/pesanan/{id}` | Pemilik, hanya bila **belum ada pembayaran** | `multipart/form-data` |","| PUT | `/api/v1/pesanan/{id}/status` | `Admin` (ubah status pengerjaan) | `application/json` |","| DELETE | `/api/v1/pesanan/{id}` | Pemilik, hanya bila **belum ada pembayaran** | — |","","\u003e Pembayaran yang dibatalkan **tetap tercatat** di tabel `payments`, sehingga","\u003e pesanan yang sudah pernah dibuatkan pembayaran tidak bisa lagi diubah (`PUT`)","\u003e atau dihapus (`DELETE`).","\u003e","\u003e `DELETE /api/v1/pesanan/{id}` adalah **soft delete**: baris `Pesanan` dan","\u003e `Pesanan_Detail` tidak dihapus permanen, hanya diisi `Deleted_At` /","\u003e `deleted_at`. Setelah di-soft delete, pesanan tidak muncul lagi di endpoint","\u003e GET mana pun dan `GET /api/v1/pesanan/{id}` mengembalikan `404`.","","## Model","","### `PesananRequest`","","Request POST/PUT dikirim sebagai **`multipart/form-data`** (bukan JSON).","Setiap parameter adalah form field terpisah dengan nama persis seperti di","bawah — termasuk field di dalam `items[N]` (index mulai dari `0`).","","```http","POST /api/v1/pesanan HTTP/1.1","Authorization: Bearer \u003ctoken\u003e","Content-Type: multipart/form-data; boundary=---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client","","---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client","Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"idAlamat\"","","5","---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client","Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"items[0].idProduct\"","","5","---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client","Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"items[0].idUkuranProduk\"","","","---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client","Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"items[0].ukuranCustom\"","","A3","---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client","Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"items[0].qty\"","","1","---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client","Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"items[0].notes\"","","Cetak warna","---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client","Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"items[0].desain\"; filename=\"desain.png\"","Content-Type: image/png","","\u003cbinary file\u003e","---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client","Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"items[0].desainText\"","","Selamat Ulang Tahun","---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client--","```","","| Form field | Tipe | Wajib | Keterangan |","|---|---|---|---|","| `idAlamat` | int | Ya | Harus milik user yang login |","| `items[0].idProduct` | int | Ya | Product harus ada. Minimal 1 item (`items[0]`) |","| `items[N].idProduct` | int | Ya | Item tambahan, index berurutan mulai `1` |","| `items[N].idUkuranProduk` | int? | Tidak | Harus milik product terkait. Kosongkan bila tidak dipakai |","| `items[N].ukuranCustom` | string? | Tidak | Ukuran bebas |","| `items[N].qty` | int | Ya | Minimal 1 |","| `items[N].width` | decimal? | Tidak | Lebar, dipakai product `PerArea`. Satuan = `product.dimensionUnit` |","| `items[N].height` | decimal? | Tidak | Tinggi, dipakai product `PerArea` |","| `items[N].length` | decimal? | Tidak | Panjang, dipakai product `PerLength` |","| `items[N].notes` | string? | Tidak | Catatan item |","| `items[N].desain` | file | Tidak | **File desain** dikirim sebagai multipart file field (bukan Base64, bukan JSON) |","| `items[N].desainText` | string? | Tidak | Teks desain |","","\u003e **Jangan kirim field `items[N].desainFilePath`.** Path file desain","\u003e (`desainFilePath`) **dibuat server** dari file multipart yang diupload","\u003e (`items[N].desain`) dan hanya muncul di response.","\u003e","\u003e `hargaSatuan`, `subtotal`, dan `totalHarga` juga **dihitung server**","\u003e berdasarkan `product.pricingMode` + konfigurasi harga. Frontend **tidak**","\u003e mengirim harga. Rincian tiap pricing mode ada di","\u003e [pricing-system.md](pricing-system.md).","","### `PesananResponse`","","```json","{","  \"id\": 4,","  \"idUser\": 2,","  \"namaUser\": \"Habib Herdiansyah\",","  \"idAlamat\": 5,","  \"alamat\": \"Koto Tuo, Payakumbuh, West Sumatra, 26218, Indonesia\",","  \"idStatusPengerjaan\": 1,","  \"statusPengerjaan\": \"Sedang Berlangsung\",","  \"totalHarga\": 100000.00,","  \"paymentStatus\": \"unpaid\",","  \"createdAt\": \"2026-09-17T08:18:46+07:00\",","  \"updatedAt\": \"2026-09-17T08:18:46+07:00\",","  \"details\": [","    {","      \"id\": 6,","      \"idPesanan\": 4,","      \"idProduct\": 5,","      \"namaProduct\": \"Bodi Bakar\",","      \"idUkuranProduk\": null,","      \"namaUkuran\": null,","      \"ukuranCustom\": null,","      \"qty\": 1,","      \"hargaSatuan\": 100000.00,","      \"pricingMode\": \"Fixed\",","      \"widthMeters\": null,","      \"heightMeters\": null,","      \"lengthMeters\": null,","      \"dimensionUnit\": \"meter\",","      \"areaM2\": null,","      \"subtotal\": 100000.00,","      \"notes\": \"Cetak warna\",","      \"desainFilePath\": null,","      \"desainText\": \"Selamat Ulang Tahun\"","    }","  ]","}","```","","`paymentStatus` bisa `unpaid` (belum ada pembayaran) atau salah satu status","pembayaran (`pending`, `paid`, `cancelled`, `expired`, dst — lihat","[README](README.md#status-pembayaran)).","","Nilai tersebut diambil dari `payments.idStatusPayment` pembayaran terakhir","pesanan (JOIN ke tabel master `status_payment`), lalu kodenya dipetakan di","C# lewat `PaymentStatusMap`. Pesanan yang belum punya baris di `payments`","tetap `unpaid`.","","Ketiga endpoint pembacaan (`GET /api/v1/pesanan`, `GET /api/v1/pesanan/all`,","`GET /api/v1/pesanan/{id}`) sekaligus menyinkronkan pembayaran yang masih","`pending` ke Midtrans (`GET /v2/{order_id}/status`) sebelum response dikirim.","Jadi status pesanan yang baru dibayar langsung berubah menjadi `paid` pada","refresh berikutnya, tanpa menunggu notifikasi webhook. Bila Midtrans tidak","dapat dihubungi, daftar pesanan tetap dikembalikan dengan status yang tersimpan","di database.","","`statusPengerjaan` bernilai `Sedang Berlangsung`, atau `Dibatalkan` bila","pembayaran pesanan dibatalkan oleh pelanggan atau kedaluwarsa (lihat","[payment.md](payment.md#post-apiv1paymentidpesanancancel)).","","---","","## POST /api/v1/pesanan","","**Content-Type: `multipart/form-data`** — JANGAN kirim JSON.","","Kirim setiap parameter sebagai form field terpisah:","","```","Content-Type: multipart/form-data","","idAlamat: 5","items[0].idProduct: 5","items[0].qty: 2","items[0].notes: Cetak warna","items[0].desain: (file, opsional)","```","","**Response 200** — `PesananResponse`.","","**Error `400`**","","```json","{ \"message\": \"Content-Type harus multipart/form-data\" }","{ \"message\": \"IdAlamat wajib diisi\" }","{ \"message\": \"Alamat tidak ditemukan\" }","{ \"message\": \"Pesanan minimal memiliki 1 item\" }","{ \"message\": \"IdProduct wajib diisi\" }","{ \"message\": \"Qty minimal 1\" }","{ \"message\": \"Produk 999 tidak ditemukan\" }","{ \"message\": \"Ukuran tidak sesuai dengan produk 5\" }","```","","```js","// Gunakan FormData agar browser/library membuat boundary multipart","// OTOMATIS. JANGAN set header Content-Type secara manual.","const form = new FormData();","form.append(\"idAlamat\", \"5\");","form.append(\"items[0].idProduct\", \"5\");","form.append(\"items[0].qty\", \"2\");","form.append(\"items[0].notes\", \"Cetak warna\");","// form.append(\"items[0].desain\", fileInput.files[0]); // file desain (opsional)","form.append(\"items[0].desainText\", \"Selamat Ulang Tahun\");","","const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/v1/pesanan`, {","  method: \"POST\",","  headers: {","    // TANPA \"Content-Type\" — browser yang set multipart/form-data + boundary","    Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,","  },","  body: form, // BUKAN JSON.stringify","});","const pesanan = await res.json(); // pesanan.id dipakai untuk pembayaran","```","","## GET /api/v1/pesanan","","**Response 200** — `PesananResponse[]` milik user login, urut `id` terbaru.","","## GET /api/v1/pesanan/all","","**Response 200** — seluruh pesanan (Admin/Petugas).","**Error `403`** — Pelanggan.","","## GET /api/v1/pesanan/history (Order History)","","Ringkasan parameter, contoh request/response, dan error ada di","[laporan.md](laporan.md#order-history). Endpoint ini mengembalikan pesanan","**aktif** (`Deleted_At IS NULL`) milik user login (pelanggan) atau seluruh","pesanan (Admin/Petugas, bisa difilter `idUser`), dengan filter:","","| Query parameter | Tipe | Wajib | Keterangan |","|---|---|---|---|","| `startDate` | string `yyyy-MM-dd` | Tidak | Rentang awal (inclusive) |","| `endDate` | string `yyyy-MM-dd` | Tidak | Rentang akhir (inclusive) |","| `idStatusPengerjaan` | int | Tidak | Filter status pengerjaan |","| `idUser` | int | Tidak | Khusus `Admin`/`Petugas`; diabaikan untuk pelanggan |","| `page` | int | Tidak | Default `1` |","| `pageSize` | int | Tidak | Default `10`, maksimal `100` |","","**Response 200**","","```json","{","  \"items\": [ { \"...\": \"PesananResponse[]\" } ],","  \"total\": 57,","  \"page\": 1,","  \"pageSize\": 10","}","```","","**Error `400`**","","```json","{ \"message\": \"Format startDate harus yyyy-MM-dd\" }","{ \"message\": \"Format endDate harus yyyy-MM-dd\" }","```","","## GET /api/v1/pesanan/{id}","","**Response 200** — `PesananResponse` (termasuk `details`).","**Error `404`** — `{ \"message\": \"Pesanan tidak ditemukan\" }`.","","## PUT /api/v1/pesanan/{id}","","Field form **sama seperti POST** (multipart/form-data, bukan JSON).","Mengganti seluruh `items` (detail lama di-soft delete, lalu detail baru","di-insert sebagai baris aktif).","","**Response 200** — `PesananResponse` terbaru.","","**Error `400`**","","```json","{ \"message\": \"Pesanan tidak ditemukan atau sudah memiliki pembayaran\" }","```","","## PUT /api/v1/pesanan/{id}/status","","**Akses: hanya `Admin`** (`403` untuk `Petugas`/`Pelanggan`).","**Content-Type: `application/json`** — bukan multipart.","","Ubah `idStatusPengerjaan` sebuah pesanan. Detail lengkap (request, response,","error) ada di [laporan.md](laporan.md#put-apiv1pesananidstatus-admin).","","**Request**","","```http","PUT /api/v1/pesanan/12/status HTTP/1.1","Authorization: Bearer \u003ctoken-admin\u003e","Content-Type: application/json","","{ \"statusPengerjaan\": \"Selesai\" }","```","","atau pakai id:","","```json","{ \"idStatusPengerjaan\": 3 }","```","","Minimal salah satu field wajib diisi, dan status harus ada di tabel","`status_pengerjaan`.","","**Response 200** — `PesananResponse` terbaru (termasuk `updatedAt`).","","**Error**","","```json","// 400 - body kosong / kedua field kosong","{ \"message\": \"IdStatusPengerjaan atau StatusPengerjaan wajib diisi\" }","","// 400 - status tidak ada di master","{ \"message\": \"Status pengerjaan 99 tidak ditemukan\" }","{ \"message\": \"Status pengerjaan 'Nganggur' tidak ditemukan\" }","","// 404 - pesanan tidak ada / sudah di-soft delete","{ \"message\": \"Pesanan tidak ditemukan\" }","","// 403 - bukan role Admin","```","","Status yang tersedia: `GET /api/v1/status-pengerjaan`","(seed: `1 Sedang Berlangsung`, `2 Dibatalkan`, `3 Selesai`).","","## DELETE /api/v1/pesanan/{id}","","**Response 200**","","```json","{ \"message\": \"Pesanan berhasil dihapus\" }","```","","**Error `400`** — `{ \"message\": \"Pesanan tidak ditemukan atau sudah memiliki pembayaran\" }`.","","\u003e DELETE di sini adalah **soft delete**: `Pesanan.Deleted_At` dan","\u003e `Pesanan_Detail.deleted_at` diisi waktu hapus; baris tetap ada di database","\u003e agar histori \u0026 laporan tetap konsisten containertiming="hpc"><article class="markdown-body entry-content container-lg" itemprop="text"><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Pesanan</h1><a id="user-content-pesanan" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Pesanan" href="#pesanan"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Base path: <code>/api/v1/pesanan</code> — semua endpoint <strong>butuh Bearer token</strong>.
<code>idUser</code> diambil dari JWT, bukan dari body.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Endpoint mana yang dipakai frontend admin dan mana yang dipakai pelanggan:
lihat <a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/docs/admin.md">admin.md</a> dan <a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/docs/pelanggan.md">pelanggan.md</a>.</p>
</blockquote>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Aturan untuk AI Agent:</strong> endpoint <strong>POST</strong> dan <strong>PUT</strong> pesanan <strong>wajib</strong>
memakai <strong><code>Content-Type: multipart/form-data</code></strong>. <strong>JANGAN PERNAH</strong> mengirim
body JSON dan <strong>JANGAN</strong> memakai <code>Content-Type: application/json</code> untuk
endpoint ini. Kirim setiap parameter sebagai form field terpisah dengan nama
persis seperti di bawah. File desain dikirim sebagai <strong>multipart file field</strong>
(bukan Base64, bukan JSON). Boundary <strong>tidak</strong> ditulis manual — biarkan
HTTP client/library yang membuatnya otomatis. Pastikan semua field wajib
tersedia sebelum mengirim request; jika ada yang kurang, tanyakan dulu
kepada user, jangan kirim request.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Ringkasan Endpoint</h2><a id="user-content-ringkasan-endpoint" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Ringkasan Endpoint" href="#ringkasan-endpoint"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Method</th>
<th>Endpoint</th>
<th>Akses</th>
<th>Content-Type</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>POST</td>
<td><code>/api/v1/pesanan</code></td>
<td>Pelanggan (pemilik)</td>
<td><code>multipart/form-data</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>GET</td>
<td><code>/api/v1/pesanan</code></td>
<td>Pesanan milik sendiri</td>
<td>—</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>GET</td>
<td><code>/api/v1/pesanan/history</code></td>
<td>Semua role — filter tanggal/status/paginasi (lihat <a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/docs/laporan.md">laporan.md</a>)</td>
<td>—</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>GET</td>
<td><code>/api/v1/pesanan/all</code></td>
<td><code>Admin</code> / <code>Petugas</code></td>
<td>—</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>GET</td>
<td><code>/api/v1/pesanan/{id}</code></td>
<td>Pemilik, atau <code>Admin</code>/<code>Petugas</code></td>
<td>—</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>PUT</td>
<td><code>/api/v1/pesanan/{id}</code></td>
<td>Pemilik, hanya bila <strong>belum ada pembayaran</strong></td>
<td><code>multipart/form-data</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>PUT</td>
<td><code>/api/v1/pesanan/{id}/status</code></td>
<td><code>Admin</code> (ubah status pengerjaan)</td>
<td><code>application/json</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>DELETE</td>
<td><code>/api/v1/pesanan/{id}</code></td>
<td>Pemilik, hanya bila <strong>belum ada pembayaran</strong></td>
<td>—</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Pembayaran yang dibatalkan <strong>tetap tercatat</strong> di tabel <code>payments</code>, sehingga
pesanan yang sudah pernah dibuatkan pembayaran tidak bisa lagi diubah (<code>PUT</code>)
atau dihapus (<code>DELETE</code>).</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>DELETE /api/v1/pesanan/{id}</code> adalah <strong>soft delete</strong>: baris <code>Pesanan</code> dan
<code>Pesanan_Detail</code> tidak dihapus permanen, hanya diisi <code>Deleted_At</code> /
<code>deleted_at</code>. Setelah di-soft delete, pesanan tidak muncul lagi di endpoint
GET mana pun dan <code>GET /api/v1/pesanan/{id}</code> mengembalikan <code>404</code>.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Model</h2><a id="user-content-model" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Model" href="#model"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><code>PesananRequest</code></h3><a id="user-content-pesananrequest" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: PesananRequest" href="#pesananrequest"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Request POST/PUT dikirim sebagai <strong><code>multipart/form-data</code></strong> (bukan JSON).
Setiap parameter adalah form field terpisah dengan nama persis seperti di
bawah — termasuk field di dalam <code>items[N]</code> (index mulai dari <code>0</code>).</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-httpspec notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="POST /api/v1/pesanan HTTP/1.1
Authorization: Bearer &lt;token&gt;
Content-Type: multipart/form-data; boundary=---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client

---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client
Content-Disposition: form-data; name=&quot;idAlamat&quot;

5
---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client
Content-Disposition: form-data; name=&quot;items[0].idProduct&quot;

5
---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client
Content-Disposition: form-data; name=&quot;items[0].idUkuranProduk&quot;


---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client
Content-Disposition: form-data; name=&quot;items[0].ukuranCustom&quot;

A3
---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client
Content-Disposition: form-data; name=&quot;items[0].qty&quot;

1
---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client
Content-Disposition: form-data; name=&quot;items[0].notes&quot;

Cetak warna
---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client
Content-Disposition: form-data; name=&quot;items[0].desain&quot;; filename=&quot;desain.png&quot;
Content-Type: image/png

&lt;binary file&gt;
---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client
Content-Disposition: form-data; name=&quot;items[0].desainText&quot;

Selamat Ulang Tahun
---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client--"><pre><span class="pl-k">POST</span><span class="pl-c1"> /api/v1/pesanan HTTP/1.1</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-v">Authorization:</span> Bearer &lt;token&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-v">Content-Type:</span> multipart/form-data; boundary=---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client</span>

<span class="pl-ii">---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-v">Content-Disposition:</span> form-data; name="idAlamat"</span>

<span class="pl-c1">5</span>
<span class="pl-ii">---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-v">Content-Disposition:</span> form-data; name="items[0].idProduct"</span>

<span class="pl-c1">5</span>
<span class="pl-ii">---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-v">Content-Disposition:</span> form-data; name="items[0].idUkuranProduk"</span>


<span class="pl-ii">---dibuat-otomatis-oleh-client</span>

<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Form field</th>
<th>Tipe</th>
<th>Wajib</th>
<th>Keterangan</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>idAlamat</code></td>
<td>int</td>
<td>Ya</td>
<td>Harus milik user yang login</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items[0].idProduct</code></td>
<td>int</td>
<td>Ya</td>
<td>Product harus ada. Minimal 1 item (<code>items[0]</code>)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items[N].idProduct</code></td>
<td>int</td>
<td>Ya</td>
<td>Item tambahan, index berurutan mulai <code>1</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items[N].idUkuranProduk</code></td>
<td>int?</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
<td>Harus milik product terkait. Kosongkan bila tidak dipakai</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items[N].ukuranCustom</code></td>
<td>string?</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
<td>Ukuran bebas</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items[N].qty</code></td>
<td>int</td>
<td>Ya</td>
<td>Minimal 1</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items[N].width</code></td>
<td>decimal?</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
<td>Lebar, dipakai product <code>PerArea</code>. Satuan = <code>product.dimensionUnit</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items[N].height</code></td>
<td>decimal?</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
<td>Tinggi, dipakai product <code>PerArea</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items[N].length</code></td>
<td>decimal?</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
<td>Panjang, dipakai product <code>PerLength</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items[N].notes</code></td>
<td>string?</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
<td>Catatan item</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items[N].desain</code></td>
<td>file</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
<td><strong>File desain</strong> dikirim sebagai multipart file field (bukan Base64, bukan JSON)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items[N].desainText</code></td>
<td>string?</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
<td>Teks desain</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Jangan kirim field <code>items[N].desainFilePath</code>.</strong> Path file desain
(<code>desainFilePath</code>) <strong>dibuat server</strong> dari file multipart yang diupload
(<code>items[N].desain</code>) dan hanya muncul di response.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>hargaSatuan</code>, <code>subtotal</code>, dan <code>totalHarga</code> juga <strong>dihitung server</strong>
berdasarkan <code>product.pricingMode</code> + konfigurasi harga. Frontend <strong>tidak</strong>
mengirim harga. Rincian tiap pricing mode ada di
<a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/docs/pricing-system.md">pricing-system.md</a>.</p>
</blockquote>

<p dir="auto"><code>paymentStatus</code> bisa <code>unpaid</code> (belum ada pembayaran) atau salah satu status
pembayaran (<code>pending</code>, <code>paid</code>, <code>cancelled</code>, <code>expired</code>, dst — lihat
<a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/docs/README.md#status-pembayaran">README</a>).</p>
<p dir="auto">Nilai tersebut diambil dari <code>payments.idStatusPayment</code> pembayaran terakhir
pesanan (JOIN ke tabel master <code>status_payment</code>), lalu kodenya dipetakan di
C# lewat <code>PaymentStatusMap</code>. Pesanan yang belum punya baris di <code>payments</code>
tetap <code>unpaid</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Ketiga endpoint pembacaan (<code>GET /api/v1/pesanan</code>, <code>GET /api/v1/pesanan/all</code>,
<code>GET /api/v1/pesanan/{id}</code>) sekaligus menyinkronkan pembayaran yang masih
<code>pending</code> ke Midtrans (<code>GET /v2/{order_id}/status</code>) sebelum response dikirim.
Jadi status pesanan yang baru dibayar langsung berubah menjadi <code>paid</code> pada
refresh berikutnya, tanpa menunggu notifikasi webhook. Bila Midtrans tidak
dapat dihubungi, daftar pesanan tetap dikembalikan dengan status yang tersimpan
di database.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>statusPengerjaan</code> bernilai <code>Sedang Berlangsung</code>, atau <code>Dibatalkan</code> bila
pembayaran pesanan dibatalkan oleh pelanggan atau kedaluwarsa (lihat
<a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/docs/payment.md#post-apiv1paymentidpesanancancel">payment.md</a>).</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">POST /api/v1/pesanan</h2><a id="user-content-post-apiv1pesanan" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: POST /api/v1/pesanan" href="#post-apiv1pesanan"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Content-Type: <code>multipart/form-data</code></strong> — JANGAN kirim JSON.</p>
<p dir="auto">Kirim setiap parameter sebagai form field terpisah:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Content-Type: multipart/form-data

idAlamat: 5
items[0].idProduct: 5
items[0].qty: 2
items[0].notes: Cetak warna
items[0].desain: (file, opsional)"><pre class="notranslate"><code>Content-Type: multipart/form-data

idAlamat: 5
items[0].idProduct: 5
items[0].qty: 2
items[0].notes: Cetak warna
items[0].desain: (file, opsional)
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Response 200</strong> — <code>PesananResponse</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Error <code>400</code></strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;Content-Type harus multipart/form-data&quot; }
{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;IdAlamat wajib diisi&quot; }
{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;Alamat tidak ditemukan&quot; }
{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;Pesanan minimal memiliki 1 item&quot; }
{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;IdProduct wajib diisi&quot; }
{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;Qty minimal 1&quot; }
{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;Produk 999 tidak ditemukan&quot; }
{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;Ukuran tidak sesuai dengan produk 5&quot; }"><pre>{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Content-Type harus multipart/form-data<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>IdAlamat wajib diisi<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Alamat tidak ditemukan<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Pesanan minimal memiliki 1 item<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>IdProduct wajib diisi<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Qty minimal 1<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Produk 999 tidak ditemukan<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Ukuran tidak sesuai dengan produk 5<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Gunakan FormData agar browser/library membuat boundary multipart
// OTOMATIS. JANGAN set header Content-Type secara manual.
const form = new FormData();
form.append(&quot;idAlamat&quot;, &quot;5&quot;);
form.append(&quot;items[0].idProduct&quot;, &quot;5&quot;);
form.append(&quot;items[0].qty&quot;, &quot;2&quot;);
form.append(&quot;items[0].notes&quot;, &quot;Cetak warna&quot;);
// form.append(&quot;items[0].desain&quot;, fileInput.files[0]); // file desain (opsional)
form.append(&quot;items[0].desainText&quot;, &quot;Selamat Ulang Tahun&quot;);

const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/v1/pesanan`, {
  method: &quot;POST&quot;,
  headers: {
    // TANPA &quot;Content-Type&quot; — browser yang set multipart/form-data + boundary
    Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
  },
  body: form, // BUKAN JSON.stringify
});
const pesanan = await res.json(); // pesanan.id dipakai untuk pembayaran"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Gunakan FormData agar browser/library membuat boundary multipart</span>
<span class="pl-c">// OTOMATIS. JANGAN set header Content-Type secara manual.</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">form</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">FormData</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"idAlamat"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"5"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"items[0].idProduct"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"5"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"items[0].qty"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"2"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"items[0].notes"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"Cetak warna"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">// form.append("items[0].desain", fileInput.files[0]); // file desain (opsional)</span>
<span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">append</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"items[0].desainText"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"Selamat Ulang Tahun"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">res</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-en">fetch</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">`<span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-c1">API</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>/api/v1/pesanan`</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">method</span>: <span class="pl-s">"POST"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">headers</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c">// TANPA "Content-Type" — browser yang set multipart/form-data + boundary</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">Authorization</span>: <span class="pl-s">`Bearer <span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-s1">token</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>`</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">body</span>: <span class="pl-s1">form</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c">// BUKAN JSON.stringify</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">pesanan</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">res</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">json</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span> <span class="pl-c">// pesanan.id dipakai untuk pembayaran</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">GET /api/v1/pesanan</h2><a id="user-content-get-apiv1pesanan" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: GET /api/v1/pesanan" href="#get-apiv1pesanan"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Response 200</strong> — <code>PesananResponse[]</code> milik user login, urut <code>id</code> terbaru.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">GET /api/v1/pesanan/all</h2><a id="user-content-get-apiv1pesananall" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: GET /api/v1/pesanan/all" href="#get-apiv1pesananall"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Response 200</strong> — seluruh pesanan (Admin/Petugas).
<strong>Error <code>403</code></strong> — Pelanggan.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">GET /api/v1/pesanan/history (Order History)</h2><a id="user-content-get-apiv1pesananhistory-order-history" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: GET /api/v1/pesanan/history (Order History)" href="#get-apiv1pesananhistory-order-history"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Ringkasan parameter, contoh request/response, dan error ada di
<a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/docs/laporan.md#order-history">laporan.md</a>. Endpoint ini mengembalikan pesanan
<strong>aktif</strong> (<code>Deleted_At IS NULL</code>) milik user login (pelanggan) atau seluruh
pesanan (Admin/Petugas, bisa difilter <code>idUser</code>), dengan filter:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Query parameter</th>
<th>Tipe</th>
<th>Wajib</th>
<th>Keterangan</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>startDate</code></td>
<td>string <code>yyyy-MM-dd</code></td>
<td>Tidak</td>
<td>Rentang awal (inclusive)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>endDate</code></td>
<td>string <code>yyyy-MM-dd</code></td>
<td>Tidak</td>
<td>Rentang akhir (inclusive)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>idStatusPengerjaan</code></td>
<td>int</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
<td>Filter status pengerjaan</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>idUser</code></td>
<td>int</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
<td>Khusus <code>Admin</code>/<code>Petugas</code>; diabaikan untuk pelanggan</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>page</code></td>
<td>int</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
<td>Default <code>1</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>pageSize</code></td>
<td>int</td>
<td>Tidak</td>
<td>Default <code>10</code>, maksimal <code>100</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Response 200</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
  &quot;items&quot;: [ { &quot;...&quot;: &quot;PesananResponse[]&quot; } ],
  &quot;total&quot;: 57,
  &quot;page&quot;: 1,
  &quot;pageSize&quot;: 10
}"><pre>{
  <span class="pl-ent">"items"</span>: [ { <span class="pl-ent">"..."</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>PesananResponse[]<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> } ],
  <span class="pl-ent">"total"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">57</span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"page"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">1</span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"pageSize"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">10</span>
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Error <code>400</code></strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;Format startDate harus yyyy-MM-dd&quot; }
{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;Format endDate harus yyyy-MM-dd&quot; }"><pre>{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Format startDate harus yyyy-MM-dd<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Format endDate harus yyyy-MM-dd<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">GET /api/v1/pesanan/{id}</h2><a id="user-content-get-apiv1pesananid" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: GET /api/v1/pesanan/{id}" href="#get-apiv1pesananid"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Response 200</strong> — <code>PesananResponse</code> (termasuk <code>details</code>).
<strong>Error <code>404</code></strong> — <code>{ "message": "Pesanan tidak ditemukan" }</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">PUT /api/v1/pesanan/{id}</h2><a id="user-content-put-apiv1pesananid" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: PUT /api/v1/pesanan/{id}" href="#put-apiv1pesananid"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Field form <strong>sama seperti POST</strong> (multipart/form-data, bukan JSON).
Mengganti seluruh <code>items</code> (detail lama di-soft delete, lalu detail baru
di-insert sebagai baris aktif).</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Response 200</strong> — <code>PesananResponse</code> terbaru.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Error <code>400</code></strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;Pesanan tidak ditemukan atau sudah memiliki pembayaran&quot; }"><pre>{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Pesanan tidak ditemukan atau sudah memiliki pembayaran<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">PUT /api/v1/pesanan/{id}/status</h2><a id="user-content-put-apiv1pesananidstatus" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: PUT /api/v1/pesanan/{id}/status" href="#put-apiv1pesananidstatus"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Akses: hanya <code>Admin</code></strong> (<code>403</code> untuk <code>Petugas</code>/<code>Pelanggan</code>).
<strong>Content-Type: <code>application/json</code></strong> — bukan multipart.</p>
<p dir="auto">Ubah <code>idStatusPengerjaan</code> sebuah pesanan. Detail lengkap (request, response,
error) ada di <a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/docs/laporan.md#put-apiv1pesananidstatus-admin">laporan.md</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Request</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-httpspec notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="PUT /api/v1/pesanan/12/status HTTP/1.1
Authorization: Bearer &lt;token-admin&gt;
Content-Type: application/json

{ &quot;statusPengerjaan&quot;: &quot;Selesai&quot; }"><pre><span class="pl-k">PUT</span><span class="pl-c1"> /api/v1/pesanan/12/status HTTP/1.1</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-v">Authorization:</span> Bearer &lt;token-admin&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-v">Content-Type:</span> application/json</span>

{ <span class="pl-ent">"statusPengerjaan"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Selesai<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">atau pakai id:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{ &quot;idStatusPengerjaan&quot;: 3 }"><pre>{ <span class="pl-ent">"idStatusPengerjaan"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">3</span> }</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Minimal salah satu field wajib diisi, dan status harus ada di tabel
<code>status_pengerjaan</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Response 200</strong> — <code>PesananResponse</code> terbaru (termasuk <code>updatedAt</code>).</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Error</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// 400 - body kosong / kedua field kosong
{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;IdStatusPengerjaan atau StatusPengerjaan wajib diisi&quot; }

// 400 - status tidak ada di master
{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;Status pengerjaan 99 tidak ditemukan&quot; }
{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;Status pengerjaan 'Nganggur' tidak ditemukan&quot; }

// 404 - pesanan tidak ada / sudah di-soft delete
{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;Pesanan tidak ditemukan&quot; }

// 403 - bukan role Admin"><pre><span class="pl-ii">// 400 - body kosong / kedua field kosong</span>
{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>IdStatusPengerjaan atau StatusPengerjaan wajib diisi<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }

<span class="pl-ii">// 400 - status tidak ada di master</span>
{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Status pengerjaan 99 tidak ditemukan<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Status pengerjaan 'Nganggur' tidak ditemukan<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }

<span class="pl-ii">// 404 - pesanan tidak ada / sudah di-soft delete</span>
{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Pesanan tidak ditemukan<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }

<span class="pl-ii">// 403 - bukan role Admin</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Status yang tersedia: <code>GET /api/v1/status-pengerjaan</code>
(seed: <code>1 Sedang Berlangsung</code>, <code>2 Dibatalkan</code>, <code>3 Selesai</code>).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">DELETE /api/v1/pesanan/{id}</h2><a id="user-content-delete-apiv1pesananid" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: DELETE /api/v1/pesanan/{id}" href="#delete-apiv1pesananid"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Response 200</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{ &quot;message&quot;: &quot;Pesanan berhasil dihapus&quot; }"><pre>{ <span class="pl-ent">"message"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Pesanan berhasil dihapus<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Error <code>400</code></strong> — <code>{ "message": "Pesanan tidak ditemukan atau sudah memiliki pembayaran" }</code>.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">DELETE di sini adalah <strong>soft delete</strong>: <code>Pesanan.Deleted_At</code> dan
<code>Pesanan_Detail.deleted_at</code> diisi waktu hapus; baris tetap ada di database
agar histori &amp; laporan tetap konsisten (lihat
<a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/docs/schema.md#4-soft-delete">schema.md</a>).</p>
</blockquote>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Setelah pesanan dibuat, lanjutkan ke <a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/docs/payment.md">payment.md</a> untuk
membuat transaksi Midtrans.</p>
<p dir="auto">File desain yang diupload via <code>items[N].desain</code> tersimpan di server dan
path-nya muncul di response <code>details[].desainFilePath</code>
(mis. <code>/images/desain/&lt;uuid&gt;.png</code>).</p>
</blockquote>

</react-app>
